## Reader recovery and Steam first data · 2026-11-14 (READER-RECOVERY / STEAM-FIRST-DATA)

Raw current request: “深度定制优化设计我们的多源补页系统这个页包括网络超时，不要一直 loading 这样用户根本不知道进度啥情况以及有个小 bug loading 的时候点不出控制台”. Follow-up with screenshot1000121717.jpg: “之后记得去修这个一直在这个状态没办法下载”; screenshot shows video104MB and “正在等待首批数据…”. The user subsequently instructed “继续”. Exact message IDs unavailable. These requirements supersede READER-LOADING-20261114's12second disclosure without a terminal wait, while preserving actual source/chapter/page identities and foreground-only reading intent.

| ID | Adopted behavior and current evidence |
| --- | --- |
| READER-RECOVERY-01 | Reuse the current page-local recovery/MotionTextButton/MotionBusyIndicator and existing source badges; no new fullscreen loading layer. A compact caption groups page/total, source, actual stage and monotonic elapsed time. Only the caption ticks. Chapter stages distinguish local lookup, manifest acquisition and position restoration; page stages distinguish existing downloads, prefetch, local decode and image request. No invented byte percentage. Status changes use the existing motion duration and respect disabled motion. |
| READER-RECOVERY-02 | After8seconds disclose retry and existing bounded source alternatives. Current-source retry keeps its slot; explicit source selection uses existing validated continuation rules. Multiple editions of one provider stay in the existing picker. Switching sources preserves the old readable page until the new manifest is ready; it does not copy uncertain page numbers between editions. Source operation error retains target source and the current-page recovery path. |
| READER-RECOVERY-03 | Chapter/position preparation has a25second foreground wait; ordinary page resolve/decode has a45second visible wait. Stop cancels this view's wait, unloads its image request and leaves shared persistent transfers with their own owner. Attempt identity includes source/headers where relevant. Superseded callbacks and cancelled effects cannot publish success/error over a replacement attempt. Source selection covers cached/stored/remote preparation on IO inside the existing25second budget and remains explicitly cancellable. |
| READER-RECOVERY-04 | Loading/failed image slots put their tap sibling above the image gesture handler, so a tap toggles the existing reader console and a drag still belongs to the list/pager. A distinct accessible console action is available in recovery immediately. Long-strip feedback follows the actual visible window rather than the middle of the full strip. Keep main's reader admission, source backoff, original-byte budgets, directions/spreads and frame-rate/interaction wiring. |
| STEAM-FIRST-DATA-01 | The exact screenshot's remote failure is unknown. Source confirms Legacy Fetch previously waited for an entire1MiB bounded block before reporting bytes and killed that whole request after60seconds; a slow advancing block could look empty and then fail. Adapt pinned tapline HttpClient to observe body reads while retaining its existing bounded Response, route, TLS, safe sink, Range/ETag/total/length checks and512MiB media limit. Do not buffer a whole media file or use preview bytes as an original. Received bytes are transport progress; final validation/import remain separate stages. |
| STEAM-FIRST-DATA-02 | Each network read/connection has a30second idle deadline, permitting a slow but continuously advancing block. Only an attempt with no received body bytes may retry the stale pooled connection; partial-response failure ends the attempt, avoiding high-watermark/stall confusion. Native progress emits the first positive sample immediately and coalesces intermediate JNI events to200ms intervals. First-data waiting in the JVM ends after30seconds; later no-progress waiting ends after45seconds, with a matching native45second guard. Parent native work remains capped15minutes by JVM, with separate bounded extraction/import; native standalone cap is30minutes. |
| STEAM-FIRST-DATA-03 | Keep the fixed preview/task/footer layout. Waiting row shows real monotonic idle seconds; after8seconds it offers retry alongside cancel. Retry cancels the old generation before starting the same item with the current route selection. A failed task releases active state and route controls, retains bytes/error for review and preserves Steam login. No automatic route fallback or claim to bypass an external VPN. Build crate/AAR/packager/Android runtime identities move together to0.1.4-f541366 so the fix cannot silently use the older optional component. |

Primary source read: UnhingedSoftware/tapline f54136611519bef50cf31336e0c28a78eb175657, especially tapline-rt-tokio/src/http.rs and tapline/src/session.rs; local maintained patch remains the distribution source, MPL-2.0 identity and notices retained. This is an adaptation of the existing client, not a new transport dependency. UI inherits the current MangaFlow recovery/Steam Ovio workspace and shared motion components; screenshot supplies the current rejected state, not post-fix visual evidence.

Evidence boundary: independent read-only source review and a bounded correction to partial-body retry; patch applicability to pinned source and source/diff integrity only. No tests, local builds, account/device/source/network probes or new gate. Preserve main2dfa9ec adaptive work and a364efd passive-network workspace. The ordinary reader and Steam source changes are implemented; compilation, actual video availability, installed console gestures, density and motion await the normal main artifact/user feedback. GuidedPanelReader has its own detection/loading path; it was inspected but its separate timeout/recovery unification is OPEN and is not claimed in this batch.

## Passive proxy recognition and compact network workspace · 2026-11-14 (NETWORK-REFINE-171–175)

Raw user request with screenshot1000121716: “关于我们网络的这个还是好好优化重新设计下吧哦对了能不能做到识别当前开启的代理”. Exact message ID unavailable. The screenshot shows a nearly full-height panel with three large route cards, repeated explanations, two address/collaboration disclosures, a lower system-state paragraph and a repeated default footer separated by large blank space. This refines NETWORK-WORKSPACE-150, retaining real routing/category/task identities and explicit capability errors; it does not authorize guessing another application's node, scanning ports or altering its settings.

| ID | Adopted behavior and observable boundary |
| --- | --- |
| NETWORK-REFINE-171 | Reuse the retained MotionDialog/PANEL, Ovio MotionTabs and MotionTabContent(fillContent=false). A520dp-wide bounded content-sized panel replaces the forced660dp body; header/close remain fixed while each scene scrolls within its real available height. Current system observation precedes one compact default-route control. Address/protocol editing appears on demand; repetitive route/collaboration/default text moves into one Info disclosure. Category overrides use compact summaries; checks use16dp status glyphs, short real results/latency and on-demand complete explanations. Controls retain44dp touch targets and font/window adaptation. Installed layout/motion remains unverified. |
| NETWORK-REFINE-172 | Android reads captured default network capabilities plus ConnectivityManager.getDefaultProxy, with handoff rechecks; distinguishes VPN, public manual HTTP endpoints and PAC without exposing PAC URLs. Default-network callbacks and protected proxy-change broadcasts notify a foreground-owned observer; release is idempotent and no new permission is added. Windows reads explicit JVM proxy properties first, otherwise bounded allowlisted HKCU Internet Settings values using fixed reg.exe arguments; iOS reads typed public CFNetwork proxy settings and releases copied resources. Unknown VPN/connectivity/automatic-discovery states stay unknown. Windows/iOS snapshots refresh on foreground/manual action; there is no invented real-time observation. |
| NETWORK-REFINE-173 | Observation is configuration only, not proxy health, source reachability, actual request route or external software/node detection. No port guessing/scanning, PAC execution, private application data, installed-app scan, credential reading or automatic network request. Manual HTTP/SOCKS endpoints retain their actual protocol; PAC is not converted into a portable fixed endpoint. Detected endpoint adoption only fills an editable draft; saving and choosing a route remain explicit. iOS HTTP settings are shown as system-only, not offered as an unsupported explicit import. |
| NETWORK-REFINE-174 | Native callbacks marshal state updates onto Main and are released when the dialog leaves foreground; read-generation guards reject stale result/error/finally writes. Previous system status can remain visible after refresh failure but cannot be adopted while loading/failed/offline or after the endpoint changes. Fill suggestions are consumed once, so cancelling/saving, switching scene or reopening cannot resurrect an old address draft. Existing route snapshots keep chosen application settings, not an invariant physical VPN/network path. |
| NETWORK-REFINE-175 | Connection checks remain user-triggered against the actual chosen service and adapters; no automatic probe on open. Active/settings/target/generation changes invalidate results and prevent applying stale recommendations. Unconfigured proxy is neutral, not a network fault; rejection/rate limiting and transport failure remain distinct. SYSTEM does not universally mean every adapter uses system HTTP/PAC: ordinary platform clients retain their selector, native Steam system sockets follow OS networking/VPN and require explicit proxy for HTTP/SOCKS, and script/iOS HTTP limits remain honestly disclosed. No silent direct fallback, new routing protocol or transport rewrite. |

Primary capability references actually read: Android ConnectivityManager.getDefaultProxy and ProxyInfo (developer.android.com/reference/android/net/ConnectivityManager and /ProxyInfo); Microsoft WinINet enabling-internet-functionality and WINHTTP_CURRENT_USER_IE_PROXY_CONFIG (learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/wininet/enabling-internet-functionality and /api/winhttp/ns-winhttp-winhttp_current_user_ie_proxy_config); Apple's public CFNetworkCopySystemProxySettings/Global Proxy Settings documentation. These describe public configuration, not permission to identify another VPN app or its private routes. Desktop missing/default-port properties are not filled by guessing. Existing JNA remains desktop-window-only; no new dependency is added for recognition.

Evidence: separate Android, Desktop/iOS, dialog/settings and diagnostics implementation domains, an independent transport audit and independent platform/UI reviews. Review fixes include stale endpoint adoption, one-time suggestion consumption and cancellation-safe read generations. Preserve concurrent main2dfa9ec experience/cache/reader scheduling changes already integrated here. Source/callback/cancellation/protocol review and git diff --check only; no tests, local builds, real requests, account/device probes or added CI gate. Root publishes one complete latest-main expected-head batch and confirms ordinary CI trigger. Compilation, device/system API acceptance, native settings reads and actual rendered density/motion remain pending the real artifact/user feedback.

## Adaptive experience delivery · 2026-11-14 (EXPERIENCE-166–173)

User supplied the full “MangaFlow 体验策略深度研究” and authorized “交给你去落地”. This supersedes EXPERIENCE-158's frozen-cache limitation and the earlier adaptive design-only status for the consumers implemented in this batch. Scope: the three existing goals, optional per-group overrides, actual platform signals, reader-first bounded work, direction/time/byte prefetch, safe live cache budgets, chapter/source scheduling and truthful diagnostics. Original image bytes, source/route identity, offline downloads, account state and existing UI materials/motion remain invariants.

| ID | Adopted implementation / observable acceptance |
| --- | --- |
| EXPERIENCE-166 | A single shared ImageLoader factory, including Android Application startup. Preserve Android's OkHttp and other platforms' Ktor transports. Mutable memory-budget wrapper trims on every write; one leased disk-cache wrapper reopens the same journal only after all snapshots/editors close. No loader replacement, parallel directory owners or blanket clearing. Pending changes remain visible while a reader retains its original-image snapshot. |
| EXPERIENCE-167 | Separate stored overrides, effective budget, actual usage and active protection reasons. Fast reduction and delayed recovery use observed OS/process signals; background lifecycle never masquerades as memory pressure. Unknown sensors remain unknown. No synthetic speed tests or hardware grades. |
| EXPERIENCE-168 | Current reading has independent image slots. Background chapters, global pages and source slots are bounded, with retained per-chapter writer ownership and frozen routes. Live limits affect future admissions; current reader/transfer bytes survive ordinary tuning. A per-page work token promotes an active/composed prefetch to foreground without lifting its sibling window budgets. Slow/error/rate-limit observations use real transfers and bounded source-local recovery. |
| EXPERIENCE-169 | Adjacent original-byte prefetch follows direction, recent page cadence and whole-spread units, bounded by request and encoded-byte budgets. Unknown sizes reserve a conservative allowance; actual transfer size feeds subsequent estimates. No speculative full-image decode. The total byte window is divided between the active adjacent/panel window and next-chapter window; streaming checks cover unknown-length bodies. Cross-chapter images retain exact source/headers and current-session cancellation guards. Native JM decryption keeps directory-only speculation until its native transport can enforce optional streaming budgets. Existing downloads alone own their missing pages and frozen routes. |
| EXPERIENCE-170 | Android window hints and Compose draw votes share the effective interaction/foreground/power/thermal policy; idle releases high-refresh votes. Manual exact-rate choices appear only for an actually supported native implementation. Desktop/iOS retain system display scheduling where exact native votes are unavailable. |
| EXPERIENCE-171 | Reuse current Ovio MotionTabs, retained SettingsDisclosure, MotionSlider and press-stretch switches. Three goals first, one compact row per domain, one expanded domain at a time; manual values appear only after opting in, with per-field reset. Cache/storage shows actual image disk and decoded memory separately from offline downloads. |
| EXPERIENCE-172 | Opt-in live diagnosis reads the attached cache and admission counters, real image/transfer durations and Android FrameMetrics when available. Presented FPS, iOS/Windows frame percentiles and unavailable sensor values are not fabricated. Show pending cache application, current limits and protection/recovery reasons. |
| EXPERIENCE-173 | Root integrates one complete batch onto latest main with an expected-head lease and confirms the ordinary CI trigger. Follow current no-tests/no-local-build/no-device-or-service-probe rules. Source review is not compilation, installed appearance, measured speedup or device acceptance. |

Primary source checks: pinned Coil 3.2.0 MemoryCache/DiskCache/EventListener contracts (both cache maxSize properties are read-only in the installed version), the existing original-byte BlackholeDecoder path, current download generation/writer/foreground-slot protocol and native platform resources. The supplied research's “mutable MemoryCache maxSize” does not apply to this pinned dependency; a narrow delegating wrapper enforces the live budget instead of assuming a setter or upgrading the dependency.

UI acceptance follows existing shared settings density, aligned icon/text axes, quiet surfaces and reversible disclosure; no extra dashboard, stacked cards or technical counters on the ordinary first level. Runtime/visual and before/after performance measurements remain pending user installation, separately from source/CI evidence.

## Native ranking association repair and readable UI · 2026-11-14 (SITE-RANK-REPAIR-20261114)

Raw user: “各站榜单这个功能 bug 很多点进去一看看了关联就成图三这样了然后再次点就进图二了以及图一这个 uiux 很恶心好吗好好做好行不行啊”. Three inline screenshots supplied; exact message ID unavailable. Visible evidence: native ranking and source-selection text compete with a full comic-page/background containing old reader controls; source choice lacks covers and repeats “关联并打开”; a later detail is an unrelated work. Do not copy private images or account contents into the repository. This is user rejection of the current native ranking UX, not acceptance of its previous source-only review.

Bounded repair design: preserve Copy 热榜, native site/kind/scope values, source registry/profile membership, downloaded bytes/progress and working auth. No composite scoring, new endpoint/dependency, global matching rewrite or authentication change.

- RANK-REPAIR-01 (ownership): source/search callbacks carry the native work key; selection must belong to the current result snapshot. A dedicated, cancellable source-open phase owns canonical catalogue validation and only then persists the association. Detach its job before handing off to the existing openManga flow. Source/page work is cancelled on navigation/search changes; a late result cannot apply to another work. Do not label the existing self-cancel observation as a reproduced sole cause: openManga's navigation update is synchronous.
- RANK-REPAIR-02 (identity/recovery): validate encoded source routing and native ID consistency, canonical source metadata against the actually selected result, active installed ordinary source and known external-ID conflicts. Version new validated associations; legacy weak manual links require re-selection instead of direct wrong-work entry, and are not bulk-deleted. Keep a visible source-change affordance and explicit local unlink. A provider failure or ambiguous response keeps the lookup page actionable rather than storing an unverified mapping.
- RANK-REPAIR-02a (uncertain matches): canonical source data alone is not proof that it is the native ranked work. A complete native title/author/ID match may open directly; otherwise disclose a single in-row actual-cover/title/version comparison and require an explicit confirmation bound to work/source/request. Keep that pane during confirmation, use the existing retained size transition, and never persist a mere search hit. New records carry identityChecked; only a still-selected canonical association can write.
- RANK-REPAIR-03 (page relationship): keep list→native work→body detail as one existing app journey with retained scroll and the real native cover transition. The body detail/reader/download/progress chain remains the existing one. Do not create body IDs from ranking IDs or merge chapter systems.
- RANK-REPAIR-04 (readability/density): a single stable high-opacity content base protects ranking text and work/source information; retain wallpaper as peripheral atmosphere. Reuse the existing PageTitleHeader, Ovio BrowseTabs, CompactGlassPopover, MangaCover/CatalogueCover and motion press roles. One primary site/header row and a compact kind strip replace the two oversized control bands. Use natural grouped title/author/metric rows,56×80dp ranking covers and a compact native-work header. Keep numeric/WORK_TITLE/READING scenes; long titles are bounded with access to full information.
- RANK-REPAIR-05 (source selection): real source thumbnail + title + source/author + one clear action. Show actual selected-source progress in its row, preserve scroll/back/search cancellation, and group provider failures into a compact retry/disclosure. No repeated per-row “关联并打开”, raw protocol paragraphs or fake availability/percent/counts. Candidate relevance is computed with the source request off Main and is only a display grouping, never identity proof. | Requirement | Adopted implementation and observable acceptance |

| STEAM-GUARD-LIFETIME-20261114 | A lifecycle ViewModel owns the actual client and nonsecret view intent. Host recreation/foreground loss no longer closes or replays the pending login; view-local password/code buffers clear on background. The native pending request reconnects with bounded retries and heartbeat, including ambiguous-submit recovery on the original challenge. Explicit cancel/mode change/dismiss still owns cancellation. This is same-process/configuration retention, not process-death or unlimited-challenge persistence. Return from mail should show the same challenge until Steam expires it. |
| STEAM-GUARD-INPUT-20261114 | Adapt the existing panel/form grammar around one real Foundation editable field and five visible alphanumeric cells: uppercase/paste/backspace/focus/IME/OTP semantics, explicit complete submission, Guard errors at the input. Mobile/email approval remains its own method. JNI retains its existing bounded compatibility check; the UI convention is not claimed to establish every Steam protocol code length. |
| STEAM-NONBLOCKING-20261114 | Separate auth, browse, transfer and optional profile lifetimes, native handles, cancellation and errors. Blank search performs no network work; login/restoration does not auto-search. Query editing, scrolling and pure previews stay usable during transfer. Browse generations and stable per-library/query/kind grid positions prevent stale results replacing the current view; transfer bytes do not key page transitions. Authenticated CM work waits in a bounded gate rather than logging on over another live account operation; anonymous public search may continue. |
| STEAM-ACCOUNT-LIBRARY-20261114 | Show verified SteamID/account fallback immediately; obtain actual persona/avatar separately, bound to that identity/credential epoch. Profile failure is optional and retryable. Browsing, favorites and subscriptions use the existing compact scene strip; collection requests call the pinned PublishedFile.GetUserFiles RPC with myfavorites/mysubscriptions and app431960, never ordinary text-search approximations. Do not label a merely saved local token as logged in. |
| STEAM-TRANSFER-PROGRESS-20261114 | Source evidence identified the old Legacy full-body Vec-before-write path as a reason0 B could persist while bytes arrived; the screenshot alone cannot identify its actual stalled stage. Replace it with existing Fetch/FileSink1MiB Range writes and actual persisted-byte events, preserving frozen network routes/TLS/content/path/size validation. One compact task footer shows waiting/connecting/resolving/downloading/verifying/importing, explicit cancel, failure/retry and success. Determinate progress requires actual known total/progress; pre-byte work stays indeterminate.60s per Legacy request and90s without material progress surface a stage error instead of an endless0 B. |

UI/UX baseline and scope: retain the established Ovio/PANEL workspace, MotionTabs/semantic page transitions, MotionIconButton/MotionTextButton feedback and Haze backdrop; no new theme or component migration. The shared header contains identity/account/close, the retained content viewport owns scroll, and the small task footer owns transfer progress. Browse/collection is the primary scene strip; image/video is a compact secondary control. Mature references actually read: Android ViewModel ownership (https://developer.android.com/topic/libraries/architecture/viewmodel.html); Android Compose progress semantics (https://developer.android.com/develop/ui/compose/components/progress); Valve's historical2021 download-design note (https://store.steampowered.com/oldnews/88497/?l=english), used for task/stage separation rather than claimed as an observed current client; pinned tapline f54136611519bef50cf31336e0c28a78eb175657 and generated RPC schema. Steam's public profile XML is explicitly deprecated/privacy-limited (https://partner.steamgames.com/documentation/community_data); its failures are nonfatal and no publisher key or private profile access is invented.

Evidence is source implementation and interface integration only. No build/test/device/browser/account/CDN/service probe was run for this batch. The expected-head source push triggers existing normal CI; it is not proof of compilation, original download success or visual acceptance. Legacy servers must support the validated Range/strong-ETag path; unsupported or changed content now fails explicitly without a full-Vec fallback.512MiB is a content/disk limit, not a measured decoder/peak-RAM guarantee. Android requires the matching same-signer optional component; Windows bundles its library; iOS continues to report unsupported.

| Finding / priority | Evidence and current decision |
| --- | --- |
| Download-time grid interaction / fixed here | SteamWallpaperBrowser derives available=false for a transfer or search, then applies motionInputGate(available) to the entire LazyVerticalGrid. That consumes scroll as well as unsafe mutations. Remove only that grid gate and allow its pure preview callbacks/press feedback. Keep search, kind changes, new download/account operations and the route chooser gated while their single client is busy; keep the existing enum transition gate, hoisted grid position and actual transfer footer. Browsing a different preview never cancels or replaces the active task. Actual phone feel remains pending. |
| Download preparation route / P0 OPEN | MangaViewModel2613–2616 fetches repository.pages before downloads.enqueue; needsSourceRefresh retry2242 has the same path. CHAPTERS snapshot is captured only later in DownloadStore.beginTransfer. Copy SourceHttp, JS getPageList and old JM pages therefore can contact the original route before the routed worker exists. This is a real code-level coverage gap, not a live endpoint reproduction. The next bounded slice should acquire one route before preparation, isolate preparation requests/sharing by that route, and persist the same snapshot into enqueue/refresh. Keep ordinary reading/browsing/auth unchanged. Merely adding coroutine context without route-scoped in-flight identity is insufficient: UnifiedMangaRepository779–798 and CopyMangaRepository284–298 merge by chapter only. |
| Legacy Steam progress / P0 OPEN | Actual pinned session.rs Legacy branch awaits Fetch::get into one complete Response.body, checks its size and only then writes FileSink. Native adapter initially emits0/total and emits completion afterwards; Legacy has no intermediate Event::Progress. Thus the progress bar can remain at0 even while bytes arrive. The payload bound512MiB is not a constant-memory guarantee. Next adapt the maintained fetch/body path to bounded streaming into the owned file with real byte progress, preserving route/TLS/cancel/path/media limits. Do not fake progress with a timer. |
| Task scope / P1 OPEN | Current visible controls configure subsequent category defaults, not one task's independent override or the actual currently connected route. Make one-off vs saved-default scope explicit and display a task's frozen choice separately from future defaults. The optional snapshot override API alone is not a user-facing task picker. |
| Steam task lifetime / P1 OPEN | Closing the Workshop panel or leaving foreground cancels non-auth work; actual chapter background scheduling does not make Steam wallpaper jobs background tasks. The stronger journey would let a real task survive the view and reserve cancellation for an explicit action, without claiming unsupported Legacy resume or iOS Steam support. |
| Acceptance boundary | Stable sizing, enum-only transitions, QR allocation and isolated transports are meaningful foundations. They do not prove phone/tablet/desktop appearance, interruption behavior, proxy reachability, progress cadence or low-memory behavior. No visual PASS or benchmark score is asserted. |

Independent source audit confirmed the first-download/refresh preparation path and classified cross-consumption of a page already requested by the reader as an existing efficiency tradeoff, not a newly introduced lifetime defect. Source/byte identity and source rate limits remain required; do not solve route coverage by duplicating every request or changing ordinary reading globally.

## Download connection diagnosis · 2026-11-14 (NETWORK-DETECT-20261114)

Current-thread user requirement (exact message IDs unavailable): “去吧但是我有问题啊外部代理控制我们软件你内部还有办法自己配置生效？”; then repeated “继续”. The preceding network-design raw wording remains at ROUTING-DETECT-20261114. This adds diagnosis to the actual DOWNLOAD-ROUTING-143 implementation on17f14a5; it does not create a second routing store or change ordinary reader/auth transports.

- Settings → 应用 → 网络与下载 → 检测连接 reuses the current MotionTabs, RouteDisclosure and MotionTextButton sources. Only explicit “检测并推荐” starts requests. Choose 漫画接口 or 更新服务, compare system/app-direct plus an already saved proxy, then separately click “用于下载默认”. Category overrides and active transfer snapshots remain untouched.
- The actual checked DownloadRouteSnapshot and NetworkClient adapters are used. Each route makes at most two small anonymous GETs, with two concurrent route checks,4s connect/6s read/8s call bounds and a256KiB body ceiling. Copy uses its existing configured default origin and stable installation-scoped public headers; no account token/password or alternate identity is submitted. GitHub uses the app's existing public releases endpoint. Expected JSON content is checked.401/403/429, unexpected redirects/content, unsupported iOS HTTP proxy and transport errors are not collapsed into “no Internet”.
- Results show average complete-interface response time. A near tie preserves the system recommendation; this is neither bandwidth measurement nor proof of a globally fastest path. No port scan, proxy software write, VPN bypass claim or automatic application of recommendations. Native Steam CM/CDN routes are not validated by these HTTPS probes.
- Closing the disclosure, leaving its retained desktop category or changing stored settings cancels the owned coroutine and invalidates its result. Both result publication and applying a recommendation check the actual current stored snapshot, preventing a delayed result from overriding a manual change. Proxy endpoint and independent categories are preserved.
- Application-direct disables the transfer's HTTP/SOCKS proxy; an external VPN/TUN may still capture it. Specifying a listener chooses where the app sends the request; that proxy's own rules still choose its final egress. A conditional external-client setup is described under DOWNLOAD-ROUTING-143, not asserted as universal physical direct access. Primary platform reference: [Android VPN guide](https://developer.android.com/develop/connectivity/vpn).

Scope/evidence: four source files plus the existing DESIGN/PROJECT-STATE records, one latest-main lease and normal CI trigger. Preserve concurrent content profiles, catalogue/comments, rankings and all DOWNLOAD-ROUTING-143 native/JS/JM/Steam/update adapters. Bounded source review found no blocking cancellation/configuration issue; no tests, local builds, service/device/account probes or measured route/speed results. The button runs its real check only when the user invokes it in the built app. Automatic environment/concurrency/cache tuning remains ADAPTIVE-EXPERIENCE-20261114 OPEN.

## Download routing implementation · 2026-11-14 (DOWNLOAD-ROUTING-143)

This implements ROUTING-DESIGN-142 after the user's “继续”; the intervening “失败了” report was independently diagnosed from actual workflow logs. Keep the raw full requirement and Steam UI/authentication decisions in STEAM-UI-141. Scope is download connection selection and its UI; ordinary reading/source browsing/auth retain their prior transports, except Steam's explicitly labelled connection setting which intentionally includes its own authentication/search/CDN job.

### Current adopted behavior and actual UI

- Settings → 应用 → 网络与下载 owns the default download mode, one explicit proxy endpoint, and progressive independent category defaults. Existing Ovio MotionTabs, Motion buttons, quiet input rows and retained MotionDialog/PANEL paths are reused. Explicitly available Haze sources keep the panel material; absent sources retain the normal existing Surface fallback.
- System network / app direct / specified proxy are different modes. Category controls can inherit; a task captures its effective route at start. The shared snapshot API additionally accepts an optional overrideMode; current visible controls configure the category default for subsequent tasks, not an invented per-running-task live switch. Download UI, fonts, models, runtime, Steam, update, WebDAV and source-import entry points expose the appropriate compact control; ongoing tasks disable their local setting control.
- “应用直连” means no app/system HTTP or SOCKS proxy for this transfer, not a guarantee to bypass another VPN, TUN or router. No whole-process network binding, global ProxySelector/env mutation, new VPN or fake bypass toggle is introduced.
- New proxy setup defaults to SOCKS5 because it supports more actual download paths. Endpoint accepts validated host/IP, port and HTTP/SOCKS type; no username/password, URL credentials or guessed working port. Missing or unsupported configuration produces a concrete error before a request. Proxy failures do not silently switch direct.
- Defaults affect new tasks and explicit manual retry. Existing chapter work IDs preserve their saved snapshot across automatic retry/restart; explicit retry cancels/joins the previous generation before using new settings. Metadata added to the existing manifest is optional and backward compatible, carries no credentials and is validated. Damaged optional routing never downgrades a completed chapter or discards files.
- A conditional setup remains possible where the external client supports both per-app exclusion and a local listener: exclude MangaFlow from that VPN/TUN capture, enter its local SOCKS/HTTP listener, and route selected downloads explicitly to it. Ordinary source browsing/reading still use their prior network and must remain reachable in such a setup; this is not automatic or universally verified bypass.

| Download route | Actual source implementation / ownership |
| --- | --- |
| Shared HTTP | DownloadRoutingStore persists application download/category defaults. NetworkClient holds one checked immutable snapshot. Android/desktop configure the actual OkHttp builder; DIRECT explicitly uses NO_PROXY. iOS configures actual SOCKS/session behavior. No route parameter preserves prior reader/auth behavior. |
| Normal chapters | The worker owns one routed NetworkClient, passes it through DownloadStore to DownloadPageTransfer, and closes it through endTransfer. Preserve current acceptCompression=false, response headers, strong ETag/Range,2 mobile/4 desktop workers, original source/page identity and persisted progress. |
| JM chapters | A chapter-owned JmDownloadSession uses the pinned5a7a4bb/v1.1.12 public configuration/context/JmApiClient constructor. Its actual HTTP client carries the route through initialization probes, settings, metadata and decoded media. Keep upstream cookie/domain/TLS/decoding behavior; reader's existing client remains unchanged. Do not set domainProbeIntervalMs(0): the real implementation schedules it without a zero guard. |
| Fonts and AI models | Each started transfer constructs its routed client within the task try/finally. Preserve manifests, verified payload/hash, cancellation, licensing and complete original font/model data. Do not freeze defaults when the store is first created. |
| Android runtime / Steam component | Each actual component transfer owns an isolated routed OkHttp client. Catalogue, archive, redirects and cancellation retain the same snapshot. Steam uses STEAM_COMPONENT, other runtime engines COMPONENTS. Original signer, version, ELF and SHA checks remain. |
| Application updates | Android HttpURLConnection check/download redirects and Range use a task-local Proxy. NonAndroid full updates own routed Ktor clients; desktop incremental manifest and Update4j streams receive the same snapshot, including a fallback to the complete package. Keep source/current-main/tag/signing/hash and platform installer guards. |
| Third-party source installation | Preserve original NO_PROXY/public DNS/IP checks and original URL Host/TLS/SNI. A bounded SOCKS SocketFactory accepts only an already-resolved verified public target IP; Android sends that numerical address to SOCKS, preventing independent proxy-side DNS from bypassing validation. HTTP proxy is explicitly unsupported here. JS browsing/auth APIs stay on their prior path; their original image downloads use CHAPTERS. Local files need no new network transfer. |
| Steam originals and connection | Extend the pinned source patch with immutable per-job NetworkRoute covering CM-directory requests, CM WebSocket/TLS and CDN/Legacy HTTP. Real async-http-proxy1.2.5 and tokio-socks0.5.3 provide CONNECT/SOCKS; keep original Rustls checks, bounded media and no direct fallback. Each job has its own route/pool and shares the original four chunk permits. Native SYSTEM retains its previous system-network behavior, not automatic Windows HTTP/PAC discovery. |
| WebDAV | Upload collection creation/PUT and download GET use one client per operation, fixed route and finally close. Existing HTTPS/credentials/path/bounds/confirmation boundaries remain. |
| Local wallpaper / PC reception | Local image/video copies and the physical-LAN PIN receiver remain unchanged; there is no meaningless Internet proxy selector for local data. |

Actual capability limits remain visible: this version has no proxy-credential form; authenticated proxies need another route. The source installer requires SOCKS for proxied public-IP enforcement. The current iOS HTTPS engine does not support the HTTP proxy path. External VPN bypass depends on that VPN/platform configuration and is not implemented as a universal capability. These paths fail explicitly, rather than displaying success or falling back unasked.

### Ownership, state recovery and source verification

Download client ownership follows the latest concurrent5c8d7872 background/Range implementation. PageTransferKey already includes chapter generation; explicit retry invalidates/join old transfers before starting a new work ID, so a changed route cannot attach a new download to an old generation. When a stopped worker still has real reader-owned requests, endTransfer lets those requests finish and defers its single transport close; background-only work stops normally. Valid local pages are checked before a routing failure falls back to the ordinary reader path. Rate-limit/source errors retain their original semantics.

Steam native/runtime/build/catalog identity is consistently0.1.2-f541366; only its spec-version line is merged into the independently changed runtime file. Continue to preserve217's account/password/Guard, QR, stable workspace and component flash fix. Route configuration failure preserves a parsed saved session/vault; canResumeSession indicates only a local candidate. “恢复登录” invokes actual account verification, and only publishAccount may show a logged-in account. New credentials, server-invalid sessions and logout retain their own cancellation/generation cleanup.

Independent source review covered actual Ktor config order and public signatures; platform resource ownership/route freezing; the pinned native patch's10-file/25-hunk counts and exact old context; and JM's genuine constructor/domain-probe paths. Review found and fixed early password clearing, stale session fallback, zero-period JM initialization and local-cache bypass; it is source evidence, not a runtime proxy/login/visual test.

Actual CI evidence before this source batch: Android0.1.217/run43562119833 assembled the APK, built native Steam, packaged components and signed successfully; its final publish was refused because a newer main existed.216/run43561109864 failed at the same exact require_current_main guard.0.1.218/run43562749212 subsequently published v0.1.218 successfully at2026-11-14T05:49:52Z; that release contains account/Guard and stable wallpaper UI, not this new proxy batch. Do not disable the guard or rerun obsolete commits to disguise this result.

Integration is one finished cloud/in-memory latest-main batch with expected-head protection, preserving0640e52a's isolated content profiles and5c8d7872's resume/parallel work. DownloadsScreen's route entry is rebased onto the exact new profile-filtered source; common Http preserves acceptCompression/downloadWithHeaders. Existing records are updated once; no status-only follow-up, local command/build/test, service/device/account probe or new approval gate. Normal new CI trigger is the delivery checkpoint. New native dependencies, route connectivity, installed UI, login/Guard and background/Range interaction remain subject to the resulting build and real user feedback.

Current-thread sources (exact message IDs unavailable): “继续以及下载费油后天下载和断点续传和多线程”, followed by “继续”. | Requirement | Adopted bounded implementation | Acceptance and limits |
| --- | --- | --- |
| DOWNLOAD-BACKGROUND-20261114 | Existing Android WorkManager publishes its existing foreground notification before initialization, chapter scanning or queue waits; real page progress replaces preparation, identical counts are skipped. Keep persisted work, network constraint, backoff, DATA_SYNC type and deliberate pause/delete. Desktop retains its actual session scheduler and resume-on-start. | Android system quotas still apply; pending workers may each show preparation. Desktop process exit stops session execution and preserves recovery. iOS retains its existing finite native background lease; an unrestricted iOS background-transfer service is not claimed. |
| DOWNLOAD-RESUME-20261114 | Retain partial image files under exact URL plus permitted-header identity, independently of writer generation. Side metadata binds original URL, filename, final HTTPS target, strong ETag and known total; original generation/workID checks still guard publication. Validate headers before opening/writing: matching206 appends;200 replaces;416/invalid range permits one clean retry; redirect/error bodies never enter images. Fix identity encoding, retain bounded streaming, close/sync before reuse, and publish only a complete validated image. Startup/refresh prune only stale request identities. | Completed pages always remain reusable. Safe byte continuation requires the actual origin's range/strong-validator support; weak/missing validators restart only the incomplete image. JM's existing whole-image decode/materialization still resumes by completed page, not scrambled-image byte ranges. No source, history, page order or original quality change. |
| DOWNLOAD-PARALLEL-20261114 | Keep a single chapter writer and use2 mobile /4 desktop background page workers, with unique next-page reservations and serialized latest-progress callbacks. Download-store transfer capacity is3/5, leaving one lane for reading an incomplete download. Reuse a same-page Deferred, count foreground consumers, cancel/join background-owned transfers on worker exit, and preserve a real foreground consumer until it leaves. | This is bounded multi-page concurrency, not parallel arbitrary byte ranges within one small image. Pause/delete still invalidate the generation and wait for old writers. Coil's ordinary reading transport remains separate; physical network bandwidth is not guaranteed reserved. |
| READER-TILES-20261114 | Share native region-decode capacity across portable zoom and vertical images: desktop2 /iOS1. Decode visible long-page bands before neighbor bands with bounded workers; return to the UI context before publishing, reject canceled windows and retain the stable Painter. | Same tile regions, sampling, pixel quality, preview, errors and zoom32-tile limit. Android's Telephoto path remains. No measured acceleration claim. |
| HISTORY-IDENTITY-20261114 | Project the chapter-history list through cached variants and SourceKeys into exact SourceChapterRef identities. Keep the newest record of an exact-source alias; distinguish same-title records from different sources in the existing metadata line. Keep the original chapter ID/cursor and all persisted per-source history. | Matching title or page count does not prove edition/source equivalence. The screenshot alone cannot establish that its two rows are the same source. No destructive migration or arbitrary title-based merge. |
| READER-LOADING-20261114 | Existing initial list/pager state already starts at the saved page; a whole-chapter URL manifest is not sequential image downloading. Fix file resources waiting for old remote prefetch; allow loading/error blanks to toggle existing chrome; hide chrome automatically only after an image is visible. After12s, disclose existing retry/source-recovery controls with a pending label while the original request continues. | No new full-screen layer, fake failure, automatic repeated redownload or source-page offset copying. Successful zoom gestures and actual progress guards remain. The screenshot's exact upstream latency/failure is unproven. |

### ADAPTIVE-EXPERIENCE-20261114 · design candidate, not shipped automatic tuning

Recommended product model: one existing-settings disclosure“体验策略”, with“自动”(default),“节能”,“流畅优先”. Only“更多” exposes cache ceilings and a truthful current reason. No device scores, fake optimization percentages or normal-user thread counters. All modes preserve the user's actual source and original image quality. The new2/4 download defaults above are implemented; the following environment controller and UI remain an explicit next implementation slice, not an enabled feature.

| Budget | 节能 candidate | 自动 candidate | 流畅优先 candidate |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Background pages | mobile1 /desktop2 | start mobile2 /desktop4, adjust slowly from actual outcomes | ceiling mobile4 /desktop8 subject to safety limits |
| Neighbor prefetch | ahead1 /behind0 | total1–4, direction and visible waiting decide | total4–8 within byte/memory budget |
| Native region decode |1 | desktop2 /iOS1; Android native Telephoto | retain these initial caps until actual evidence supports change |
| Refresh hint | release sustained peak preference; system touch boost remains | High for interaction/scroll; no idle redraw loop | High preference with native display synchronization |
| Cache | smaller initialization budget | actual process budget and free storage | higher user ceiling with protected download/system headroom |

Controller contract: current-page loading never waits for sensing or a speed test. Learn only from real uncached request bytes/time, first-byte wait, cache hits and decode/current-page readiness; each source owns its own congestion/backoff. Unknown paths, including a native source without these measurements, keep conservative defaults. Increase one lane only after stable healthy observations;429/503, repeated timeouts, memory pressure or serious heat reduce optional admissions first. Change only future admissions; never replace an in-use Semaphore, restart a visible image or invalidate an in-flight file because a profile changes. Disk pressure evicts only rebuildable cache and stops speculative work; imported books, offline chapters, exact download checkpoints and open image snapshots are protected. Candidate initialization disk ceiling is min(user ceiling, about1% of currently free space, space remaining after safety headroom), initially capped at512MiB mobile /2GiB desktop; no mandatory minimum forces allocation on a nearly full drive.

Runtime boundaries: pinned Coil3.2.0 MemoryCache/DiskCache maxSize are read-only; memory trimToSize can reclaim rebuildable entries, but disk ceiling changes need safe initialization/next startup, never two DiskCache owners or repeated SingletonImageLoader replacement. Android34+ no longer sends some old running-low-memory trim levels, so an eventual controller must use actual process/memory information plus valid thermal/power events; iOS uses native power/thermal state. There is no verified cross-platform presented-frame telemetry here: frame-clock callbacks cannot be reported as actual FPS. These limits are part of the design rather than fabricated automatic optimization.

Primary references: [RFC9110 If-Range/ranges](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc9110.html#name-if-range), [Android long-running workers](https://developer.android.com/develop/background-work/background-tasks/persistent/how-to/long-running), [Android foreground-service timeouts](https://developer.android.com/develop/background-work/services/fgs/timeout), and agent-reviewed fixed [Coil3.2 MemoryCache](https://github.com/coil-kt/coil/blob/3.2.0/coil-core/src/commonMain/kotlin/coil3/memory/MemoryCache.kt), [DiskCache](https://github.com/coil-kt/coil/blob/3.2.0/coil-core/src/commonMain/kotlin/coil3/disk/DiskCache.kt), [EventListener](https://github.com/coil-kt/coil/blob/3.2.0/coil-core/src/commonMain/kotlin/coil3/EventListener.kt). Reuse the existing downloader, Ktor/Okio storage, WorkManager, reader controls and shared styles; no new dependency or download framework.

### ROUTING-DETECT-20261114 · network design, implementation remains OPEN

Current-thread user requirement (exact message ID unavailable): “以及对于网络相关我希望其实就是直连和代理吗代理分为外面的代理软件但得让用户给他配置成这个软件走全局是不是？以及代理地址配置这样？然后我们做个按键自动检测来帮用户配置好最佳的”. This refines, and does not close, existing ROUTING-DESIGN-142. No transport adapter, route setting or diagnostic button is shipped in this batch.

Use one progressively disclosed “连接方式” row: “跟随系统” (default), “应用直连”, “指定代理”. Following the system means the actual OS route; an external proxy needs MangaFlow included in its applicable per-app/rule route, not necessarily device-wide global mode. Application-direct bypasses an application-layer proxy only; a system VPN/TUN can still intercept it, and lockdown can forbid bypass. Do not claim to rewrite external Clash rules or system VPN policy. Specified-proxy configuration exposes only protocols supported by each real platform adapter, its endpoint and optional credentials through secure storage. No inert protocol choices or unsupported native-channel success states.

Keep one application default. Download/task details disclose an “继承默认” override only when requested; catalogue/images, offline chapters, fonts/models/extensions/components/updates and native Steam/JM must each map to their actual transport before claiming coverage. Preserve source headers, Range encoding/validators, writer generations and publication guards. Routing changes apply to future admissions; an in-flight visible image or download checkpoint retains its original client identity.

One explicit “检测并推荐” action considers only configured/available paths; no arbitrary localhost port scan, discovery of private network services or invented proxy endpoint. Issue bounded, anonymous requests to relevant public service endpoints without account credentials; validate the expected response, distinguishing403/429/challenge responses from transport failure. Compare reachability, first-byte latency and repeatability; a small probe does not measure full throughput or prove a globally best route. Candidate limits are two parallel paths, short timeouts and small traffic, subject to later calibration; subsequent actual transfers feed any adaptive tuning. Results show the recommended route and concise evidence/error. A separate “应用建议” action saves the selected scope, preserving manual choices until the user opts in. Never silently change another proxy application's global settings.

Product wording should say “当前来源推荐连接”, not imply knowledge of every VPN/system setting. Missing native-adapter observability keeps an explicit unsupported state. Implementation order: shared route model and actual platform/native adapters, then route-aware task identity and settings, then bounded diagnosis/recommendation UI. This batch delivers the design only.

Primary platform reference: [Android VPN guide](https://developer.android.com/develop/connectivity/vpn) and [VpnService.Builder](https://developer.android.com/reference/android/net/VpnService.Builder) for per-app routing and VPN bypass constraints.

Evidence/scope: source inspection and separate bounded protocol/cancellation review only. Root combines seven loading/download source files, chapter-history projection and two reader recovery files into one latest-main lease-protected batch, preserving concurrent comments, source/Steam/font/high-refresh changes. No tests, local files/commands/builds, benchmark, user-device/service probes or additional release gate. Keep current workflows/signing/current-main protections; CI trigger, completed build and installed behavior remain separate evidence. Source implementation does not close actual latency, background reliability, battery use or visual acceptance.

## Steam authentication, stable wallpaper workspace and download routing · 2026-11-14 (STEAM-UI-141 / ROUTING-DESIGN-142)

Raw user requirements in this thread (exact message IDs unavailable): “以及啊关于网络代理这块我希望每个下载相关的都得够直接配置绕过应用代理直接直连和使用代理？因为我们软件外一般还有个代理软件但那个代理软件肯定是对整个软件做代理你好好设计下 / 以及 steam wallpaperengine 那个 uiux 重新设计下现在这样真的不行啊”; then “而且 steam 创意工坊那不能通过账号密码登录吗而且那个 uiux 一直在抖啊”. Scope: a concrete complete download-routing design plus the actual Steam authentication and wallpaper UI correction. The routing controls/transport implementation below are still OPEN; do not mistake this design for an implemented all-download proxy feature.

### STEAM-UI-141: account login and stable task layout

| Decision / adopted source | Source implementation and observable acceptance |
| --- | --- |
| Actual account login | Pinned tapline f541366 already supplies Session.password_key, begin_password_login, submit_guard_code and poll_login. Add a single bounded native password-login job, RSA through that actual API and a one-entry job-bound Guard submission channel. Server-returned methods alone enable email code, authenticator code, mobile approval or email confirmation. Wrong/expired codes remain in the same pending session; do not replay the password. Keep QR, verified content session, original secure vault, cancellation and generation/handle guards. iOS stays explicitly unsupported. |
| Credential lifetime and component identity | UI account form is transient, never saveable; submit clears the password text and submitted array. The JVM synchronously takes an owned copy before returning, then clears it on request construction, cancellation, preparation failure or a launch that never starts. New loggedIn events must provide their own token fields, never borrow a prior account session. Component/build/catalog/Android spec identity is consistently0.1.1-f541366, with a new JNI nativeSubmitGuard entry. Existing signer/version/ELF/hash/vault guards remain. |
| Stable workspace / UIX-01–03 | Reuse the current Ovio MotionTabs/MotionTabContent, MotionDialog, Motion buttons, PANEL Haze and QRose; no new default-widget family or dependency. Workshop uses a bounded90%-available-height workspace (720dp cap,864dp outer width including margins), fixed task header, flexible content and one reserved font-scaled64dp status/action region. PC pairing uses84%/520dp and624dp outer width. Header and footer stay outside the single scrollable PC body. Live results, progress, QR arrival and error text cannot change the panel height. |
| Task-first hierarchy / UIX-04–06 | Browse presents search, a compact image/video selector and the original preview gallery. Phone tile minimum124dp preserves two columns at normal320/360 widths; wider workspaces use150dp, with font scaling respected. Click a tile to preview, then explicitly download/apply the original media. Header login opens the account form; QR is the other deliberate tab. Account details/logout and manual PC IP appear only in their own context. Preserve shared typography, source colors, quiet neutral surfaces, existing glass roles and44dp actions. |
| Semantic motion / UIX-07 | WorkshopPane enum alone owns the page transition. Transfer data no longer targets AnimatedContent or its natural-height SizeTransform; QR gets its full-size area before its URL arrives. Keep one hoisted grid scroll owner when leaving for preview/login and when adding more results. Backend checks the installed native library locally before publishing preparation: ordinary installed searches/downloads/logins never flash the component page. Preserve current Ovio transition/reduced-motion/input gating. |
| States and recovery / UIX-08–09 | Keep true preparation/download progress, cancel, local errors, explicit retry and actual import feedback in the status slot. Authentication approval survives switching to Steam on the same phone. Close clears transient credentials/cancels owned work while retaining the outgoing panel's data for the exit. PC discovery/PIN/key pin/physical-LAN routing remain; its header no longer scrolls away or follows discovery/receive height. Browsing uses the backend's real append-only “加载更多”, removing unsupported previous-page navigation; adding results does not jump to the top. |
| Responsive and evidence / UIX-10 | Bound against the Dialog's real safe/IME constraints, use normal text scaling and scroll form/body within the available area; retain desktop pointer/keyboard controls and existing platform ownership. Current interface evidence is source inspection and the user's jitter report; no current screenshot/video or runtime reproduction was available/authorized. Implementation is not a visual/hand-feel PASS. |

The code-supported jitter paths were natural-height centered dialogs, transient feedback insertion/removal and installed operations briefly publishing component preparation. These are concrete source causes addressed by this batch, not a claim that every observed GPU/frame issue has been reproduced. New browse/preview/login hierarchy retains original source/media identities; WallpaperStore.importMedia already applies validated real media. Previews never become imported payloads, and Scene/Web/Application are not relabelled as images.

### ROUTING-DESIGN-142: every download has an effective route, without a fake VPN bypass

**Recommended interaction.** Settings → 网络与下载 owns the application default, configured proxy endpoints and a progressive “按下载类型设置” list. Default is system. Each download/prepare surface exposes one compact “线路 · 跟随默认（系统）” action; its menu can inherit, use the system path, bypass app/system HTTP proxy, or select a configured proxy. Keep task actions and real byte progress together; do not add an equal-weight card or a new settings dialog at every download click. Show the effective selection and unsupported/blocked status. Local URI imports have no network choice; PC reception says “局域网”.

| Route mode | User-visible meaning and boundary |
| --- | --- |
| 系统网络 | Use the supported platform's current system proxy/network behavior. This is not guaranteed direct access. |
| 应用直连 | Do not use the application or system HTTP/SOCKS proxy for this transfer. It can still traverse an external VPN/TUN/router. |
| 指定代理 | Use the explicitly selected HTTP/SOCKS endpoint through the real transport adapter; do not silently fall back to direct. |
| Inherit | The effective route is task override > category default > application default. Show the resolved result beside the inherited label. |

All category defaults initially inherit; avoid asking users to configure every type. Categories must cover manga originals (including the separate JM path), Steam wallpaper media, images/video downloads, fonts, AI models/runtime/Steam components, application updates (full and incremental), extension/repository downloads, and WebDAV transfers. Browsing/auth/control traffic follows its own clearly documented source/session setting rather than changing implicitly when a large-file task changes route.

For a truly usable external-proxy combination, where the external client supports both application exclusion and a local HTTP/SOCKS listener, provide setup guidance: exclude MangaFlow from that client's VPN/TUN capture, configure its local proxy endpoint once in MangaFlow, then send only the selected tasks to that endpoint. Direct tasks can then use the ordinary network, while proxied tasks deliberately enter the external client's listener. This is a conditional integration design, not a universal bypass capability or an automatically detected/verified setup. Do not guess the user's listener port or modify their external proxy app.

“绕过外部 VPN” is a separate advanced capability, not the definition of DIRECT. Android's VPN owner must allow bypass; bind only the current transfer's socket and DNS when supported. Never change the app-wide process network to satisfy one task. Always-on/blocked-outside-VPN or unsupported configurations report that direct bypass is unavailable. Windows TUN/force-tunnel routes likewise are not bypassed by NO_PROXY. Offer relevant external split-routing guidance rather than show a false success indicator.

**Transfer consistency.** Snapshot the resolved route, endpoint credentials reference and capability when a task starts; keep metadata, redirects, chunks and Range requests on it. New defaults affect new tasks. Existing transfers offer explicit “切换线路并重试”; preserve only the verified resume behavior of that actual downloader, not invented Steam Legacy resume. Proxy failure stays on the selected route; user chooses another route explicitly. Keep route-specific pools and cancellation owners separate from reading/auth jobs. Store endpoint credentials in the existing secret store, not URLs/logs/history.

Actual source audit at c234a9a1:

| Adapter that must be implemented | Current exact path |
| --- | --- |
| Shared Ktor | net/Http.kt + platform Http actuals; ordinary DownloadPageTransfer, FontStore, PanelModelStore, WebDavBackupStore, full nonAndroid updates. There is currently no application proxy policy. |
| JM | DownloadStore decryptedJmPageBytes → JmPlatform.jvm.kt / JmApiClient.fetchImageBytes; cannot assume it uses the ordinary page client. |
| Optional Android components | reader/panels/PanelRuntimeStore.kt independent OkHttp, including SteamWallpaper.android's component loader. |
| Android and desktop incremental update | UpdateManager.android.kt HttpURLConnection; NativeIncrementalUpdate.desktop.kt Update4j openDownloadStream/officialConnection. Only nonAndroid full package uses the shared client. |
| Third-party source install and JS | SourcePluginStore.android.kt and JavascriptSourceRuntime.android.kt have explicit NO_PROXY and validated public DNS/IP ownership. Do not simply remove NO_PROXY: a proxy's independent DNS resolution can bypass the current address validation. Preserve that policy in a bounded compatible adapter or expose honest unsupported status. |
| Native Steam media / CM | Pinned tapline-rt-tokio/src/tls.rs directly TcpStream::connects; HTTP, CM list and CM WebSocket ultimately use it. Shared::new privately builds its HTTP client; Kotlin proxy settings or HTTP_PROXY do not configure this path. Needs a bounded upstream connector extension covering CM/CDN/DNS before “specified proxy” can be offered as working. |
| Local PC/import | WallpaperPc.android already binds owned LAN sockets; preserve that path. WallpaperStore local import has no download route. |

Primary references actually inspected: Android VpnService.Builder allowBypass (https://developer.android.com/reference/android/net/VpnService.Builder), Android Network API (https://developer.android.com/reference/android/net/Network), Microsoft VPN routing (https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/security/operating-system-security/network-security/vpn/vpn-routing), Ktor3.6.1 proxy support (https://ktor.io/docs/client-proxy.html), tapline f541366 session.rs / shared.rs and tapline-rt-tokio TLS connector. Ktor's current Darwin HTTPS-over-HTTP-proxy limitation must remain visible in platform capability handling; do not promise identical proxy transports on all targets.

Delivery: cloud/in-memory source integration only, one complete latest-main batch with an expected-head lease; keep all concurrent comment/detail/font/source/desktop changes and normal CI guards. No tests/local build/device/browser/account probes, no new review/approval gate. Source auth/UI is implemented; compiled/native/login/installed visuals remain pending normal CI and user feedback. ROUTING-DESIGN-142 is design complete with the above actual transport gaps still OPEN.

## High-refresh rendering and frame budget · 2026-11-14 (HIGH-REFRESH-137–140)

User request in this thread: “你看看能不能提高下帧率和流畅度呢手机一般都是至少 120hz 了电脑能多高多高电脑很多都是 300hz”. Adopt device-driven high-refresh hints and remove demonstrated redundant work; a preferred refresh rate is not measured rendered FPS. Keep the current shared app, accepted glass/motion/geometry, original image quality, exact source/chapter/progress identities and READER-RETURN-136.

| ID / task | Implementation and source reuse | Observable boundary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| HIGH-REFRESH-137 / Android cadence | While MainActivity is resumed, request the maximum reported refresh rate among modes at the current physical resolution through preferredRefreshRate; do not set a mode ID or change resolution. Re-evaluate on resume, focus return and configuration changes, write only when the preference changes, and restore the prior hint on pause. The existing mobile scene also uses Compose1.12.2 preferredFrameRate(FrameRateCategory.High) for per-draw layer votes. | No fixed60/120 cap, per-frame display queries, custom timer or artificial redraw loop. Android retains display/power/thermal arbitration; a hint cannot guarantee its acceptance or frame completion. |
| HIGH-REFRESH-138 / iPhone cadence | Add CADisableMinimumFrameDurationOnPhone=true to the actual XcodeGen info properties and reuse that same mobile Compose high category. | Enables ProMotion timing hints on supported iPhones; preserve ordinary displays, iPad and native lifecycle. No second CADisplayLink or forced timer. Actual phone timing remains unobserved. |
| HIGH-REFRESH-139 / scrolling and tiles | Pass the detail dock's continuous collapse state as a provider, read only in the existing restartable dock. Keep one OriginalTilePainter for the current preview/size/tile State and read tile lists only in onDraw. | Remove the parent's pixel-by-pixel collapse subscription and tile-arrival-driven zoom/painter rebuilding. Keep the same reversible geometry, animation curves, original pixels, visible/neighbor tile set, cancellation and resource ownership. |
| HIGH-REFRESH-140 / desktop reading | Use the pinned Compose1.12.2 official BufferedImage.toComposeImageBitmap for8bit-or-less sRGB regions, avoiding PNG encode/Skia decode; keep the existing codec path for other color spaces/depths. Desktop native VSync and display pacing remain. | No new FPS ceiling, VSync disabling, unpaced300/999 loop or speculative engine flags. System-selected monitor mode and actual frame budget govern achievable cadence; high-refresh performance is not yet measured. |

Primary references actually read: [Android ARR](https://developer.android.com/develop/ui/views/animations/adaptive-refresh-rate), [Android frame-rate/window guidance](https://developer.android.com/media/optimize/performance/frame-rate), [Apple ProMotion](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/quartzcore/optimizing-iphone-and-ipad-apps-to-support-promotion-displays), [Compose iOS frame-rate configuration](https://kotlinlang.org/docs/multiplatform/whats-new-compose-190.html#frame-rate-configuration), [pinned FrameRate source](https://github.com/JetBrains/compose-multiplatform-core/blob/v1.12.2/compose/ui/ui/src/commonMain/kotlin/androidx/compose/ui/FrameRate.kt), [pinned desktop converter](https://github.com/JetBrains/compose-multiplatform-core/blob/v1.12.2/compose/ui/ui-graphics/src/desktopMain/kotlin/androidx/compose/ui/graphics/DesktopImageConverters.desktop.kt), and [deferred Compose state reads](https://developer.android.com/develop/ui/compose/performance/bestpractices#defer-reads). Reuse existing compiled APIs without a dependency upgrade. These document API behavior and source mechanisms, not our measured performance.

Scope/evidence: cloud connector source/diff review only. Three independent source domains examined desktop cadence, shared motion and reader tiles; root integrates their bounded changes and the native cadence hints in one latest-main lease-protected batch. No tests, local files/commands/builds, benchmarks, device/browser/service probes or new validation gate. Preserve concurrent Android image-backend/R8 repair at a30525a and normal compile/package/signing/current-main guards. Main CI trigger, successful build and installed refresh/frame-time/user experience remain separate; no120/300 FPS result is asserted.

### STEAM-DIRECT-126: actual implementation, separate from PC PIN transfer

Adopt the pinned tapline core f54136611519bef50cf31336e0c28a78eb175657 through a small JNI bridge. Actual QR login verifies a Steam client session before passing the refresh token to the existing AndroidKeystore/WindowsDPAPI vault. No default plaintext TokenStore is used; errors are fixed codes/messages, secrets never enter logs or visible state. The friendly login device label is MangaFlow; upstream OS metadata/protocol remain unchanged. Local cancellation/close and logout own job/credential cleanup; a bounded login survives the explicit Steam-app/browser handoff.

One compact Workshop origin action in existing wallpaper settings opens a PANEL/MotionDialog using the current Ovio tabs/buttons/motion. Actual locally encoded QRose1.3.1 QR, official returned same-phone URL, optional-component preparation progress, image/video browsing/pagination, true download byte progress/cancel and actual import feedback share the same panel. Merely composing/opening it does not download a component or run a search. Closing cancels operations immediately and retains the outgoing content through the existing exit animation. Existing PC PIN/local import/wallpaper actions remain.

The native core queries actual app431960 rows, uses the real Video tag, and identifies a standalone image only from genuine Legacy primary filename/content metadata. Scene/Web/Application previews are never called original media. Static-image browsing can legitimately have no directly importable entries. Download details must retain app/item identity and account permission. SteamPipe manifests are bounded to512MiB/512 entries before content files are written, including paths/chunks and rejection of links/executables. Only project.json's declared real image/video main file or native-identified Legacy primary file can reach WallpaperStore after canonical-path/signature/decoder validation. No Scene renderer or asset library is added.

Android downloads a per-ABI same-APK-signer verified optional Steam library only on explicit action; existing signed catalog/SHA/ELF/managed-store machinery is reused. Existing old2/3-engine same-signer receipts remain usable; new catalogs include all4 engines and4 ABIs. No Steam native binary is linked into the base APK. Windows packages a platform DLL and lazily extracts it under a hash identity, checks it and loads on demand. iOS clearly reports unsupported.

CI fixes sources atRust1.88.0/NDKr27c/tapline commit, applies only the committed bounded/branding patch, carries upstream Cargo.lock selections into the adapter, builds4 Android ABIs with16KiB ELF alignment and a Windowsx64 static-CRT DLL, preserves the resolved lock/metadata/provenance/licenses and adds real component assets to the existing signed release. QRose1.3.1 is compatible with current Kotlin2.4.21/Compose1.12.2. No new tests or local build gate is added.

Important actual implementation limits: the older Legacy HTTP API buffers its accepted body;512MiB is an input cap, not a peak-RAM guarantee. SteamPipe uses chunked bounded concurrency. Upstream blocking work may briefly finish after cancellation, so the client uses unique non-reused owned temporary directories. Source existence is not a measured memory/performance/interop result.

The old radial score plate is replaced by the same actual-window Haze focus used for source choices, with sharp items and a reversible single progress. Horizontal, vertical and local paths use this same chain. Preview/viewport/tile dimensions remain display hints and are never advertised as original resolution. Foreign chapter manifests used16 striped locks, coupling unrelated covers/chapters. Adapt Copy's existing per-key live Deferred/readers pattern for exact foreign source/work/chapter, with generation fences, cache bounds/TTL and last-consumer cancellation. | Visible images get priority; identical reader/download/preview consumers share the same live request, including a shared failure. No quality reduction or enlarged decode window is used as a speed claim. |

Move those cached projections to Default and publish usable reader/directory state before the existing save. | Book entry and adjacent-chapter availability need not wait for that disk write; existing persistence, errors, full-directory requirements, current-request guards and original history/download/source IDs remain. |

## FONT-SCENES-100 · role-scoped optional fonts · 2026-11-14

Raw user (exact message IDs unavailable): “去吧交给你了设计好直接去落地如果是同类的可以做在设置里切换？但字体一般比较大吧如果比较大就弄成可选下载注意像你说的有些场景用什么字体所以这个配置不是全部替换只替换某个场景的”. This authorizes implementation and supersedes TYPE-86's proposal-only state. The user also asked why the already-approved font collection was not used; the actual current typography Page page_e4d707779d6081919403cd3031586676 was read, including its18-family/32-face/250-preview-character limits.

Product/UI decision: one existing “字体” inline SettingsDisclosure under appearance, followed by four independent role rows. Only one role's candidates expand at a time; no new modal/window, global-font replacement, font-size control suite or nested settings card. Every role defaults independently to system; “跟随界面” is an explicit additional choice for non-interface roles. Changing a work-title/reading/number choice never writes another role. Keep real typography scaling and existing compact UI; original manga image pixels are outside the font system.

| Role | Scope | Available choices |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Interface | Navigation, controls, chapter labels and settings | System / Noto Sans SC |
| Work title | Actual book names in discovery/search/carousel/library/history/download/source candidates/detail/reader | System / follow interface / Noto Sans SC / Noto Serif SC / LXGW WenKai |
| Reading prose | Short/full introductions, comments/replies and reading comment previews | System / follow interface / Noto Sans SC / Noto Serif SC / LXGW WenKai |
| Numbers | Native scores/heat, ranks, reading duration, page indicators and download/progress labels | System / follow interface / Inter / Geist |

Latin numeric families are not claimed to contain Chinese. Mixed labels retain UI words and units while explicit numeric runs get the chosen numeric face/tnum. Source pictograms and artwork stay intact. Actual title text measurement uses its chosen family; prose and numeric leaf nodes opt into their own roles rather than replacing MaterialTheme's whole typography with a reading or display face.

Resource decision: every custom font is optional, including the small Latin choices, so no TTF/OTF is bundled in APK/EXE/iOS. Fresh/offline launch has system text without a download. Full upstream TTFs are fixed by commit, exact byte count and Git blob SHA1; never deploy the old250-character specimens. Noto Sans SC17,772,300B, Noto Serif SC25,125,512B, WenKai Regular25,928,940B, Inter876,576B, Geist169,056B. WenKai is the single true400 face, not a pretend variable family. MiSans remains an external comparison candidate, without an unverified download entry.

Settings show per-resource size before download, actual progress/cancel, retryable error, downloaded real-family samples, and deletion. “下载并使用” applies only after verified publication and only to still-pending requested roles. Deletion resets only roles referencing that resource. Installed-file loss/parse failure falls back to system with an explicit status. Full per-family OFL/copyright text is embedded and readable offline in the inline permission disclosure; source links are user-operated.

Implementation: existing NetworkClient/StorageFile/Preferences plus one IO command owner in FontStore; streamed bounded HTTPS from pinned raw files with a jsDelivr mirror for supported-size files, no cookies/Play Services/system-font installation. An attempt owns staging files; byte length, sfnt header, Git blob prefix digest and platform font parsing precede installed/application state. Download cancellation/removal/new scene choices cannot let stale completion overwrite them. Android preloads nativeTypeface objects; nonAndroid shares Skia byte-array fonts and explicit variable weight axes400/500/600/700. Desktop outer theme now receives its actual context before font settings are read.

Type baseline: common roles converge to12/14/16/20/24sp and400/500/600 weights, normal Chinese tracking; dense prose14/22, comfortable/full prose16/26, work hero20/28 in short viewports or24/32 normally. Main metric values and denominator stay at least12sp. Narrow heat labels may drop a fractional万/亿 suffix for compact display; full native values remain in semantics/source details. Overflow sources retain the existing stack. Preserve current detail scroll plane/reader direct-download changes98/99 and screenshot91–97.

Validation boundary: source authoring/consistency and the existing normal CI only. No tests, local builds, product/browser/device probes or extra review gate. Font assets are verified upstream metadata; this is not a claim that downloads, rendering, cancellation, memory use or glyph coverage have been experienced on a device. Keep package compilation, actual native behavior and user visual acceptance separate. First device path: Settings → Appearance → Fonts, download/use a role candidate, return to the matching actual page, change another role, cancel/remove and restart offline. See third_party/fonts/ADAPTATION.md for fixed source/loader API evidence.
